## T9 casts

One row per cast of the census AFTER-T8 (103), at `ec3d08a2`; lines unchanged at this commit. Fields, ` | ` separated: id, form, path:line, cast, removal trial, verdict and reason. Removal trial: that cast alone removed in a scratch copy, its unit compiled with its `opt`; never the gate, it only backs the reason. Obj compare ignores the COFF timestamp. 'obj same' lines were rerun with `/DNON_MATCHING`, since a line that gives the unit no code (guarded, an unused inline, an uninstantiated template) gives the same obj. Pointer types are read from the PDB with `pdb_types class`, `pdb_types locals`.

Result: 0 struct members, 105 stay (103 census rows, 2 extra). No census row is a member access written as an offset: each is a downcast, a void payload, a position read at run time from a PDB member, a handle or value, or library or upstream text. Converting one would be an offset fit (D78). No `src` change.
